##### 重要提示

公司董事会及董事、高级管理人员保证本报告及摘要所载资料不存在任何虚假记载、误导性陈述或者重大遗漏，并对其内容的真实性、准确性和完整性承担个别及连带责任。

公司年度财务报告已经山东汇德会计师事务所有限公司根据财政部颁布的《企业会计准则（2006）》审计，并出具了标准无保留意见的审计报告。

本年度报告及摘要中比较列报的以前年度财务数据已经山东汇德会计师事务所有限公司根据财政部颁布的《企业会计准则（2006）》进行审计调整。

本年度报告及摘要中列报的财务数据为合并报表并经审计后的数据，相关监管指标按照母公司经营数据计算得出。

公司董事长李敏实、行长谭乐清以及财务负责人段霞，保证年度报告中财务报告的真实、完整。

莱商银行股份有限公司董事会

# Statement of Fiduciary Responsibility

The Board of Directors, the directors and senior management of the Company undertake that there are no misrepresentation, misleading statement, or material omission in the Report and its summary, and accept joint and several liability for the authenticity, accuracy and completeness of the contents of the Report.

The annual financial report of the company has been audited by Shandong Huide Certified Public Accountants Ltd. according to the Accounting Standards for Business Enterprises (2006) promulgated by the Ministry of Finance and a standard unqualified audit report has been issued.

The financial data of previous years listed in this annual report and its summary for comparison have been audited and adjusted by Shandong Huide Certified Public Accountants Ltd. according to the Accounting Standards for Business Enterprises (2006) promulgated by the Ministry of Finance.

The financial data listed in this annual report and its summary are the audited data of consolidated statements and relevant regulatory indicators have been calculated based on the operational data of the parent company.

Chairman Li Minshi, President Tan Leqing and Finance Director Duan Xia confirm the authenticity and completeness of the financial statements in the Annual Report.

Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.

## 七、公司各类风险和风险管理情况

董事会及其专门委员会、高级管理层及其专业委员会、风险管理部门和相关业务部门、风险报告员等构成公司风险管理的组织架构。公司董事会承担风险管理的最终责任。风险管理委员会在董事会授权下开展工作并对董事会负责，负责公司风险管理政策、程序、量化标准和重大风险解决方案的审议和制定。监事会负责监督董事会和高级管理层在风险管理方面的履职情况。高级管理层负责风

险管理政策的具体实施，设立了信贷审查委员会、资产负债管理委员会等专业委员会，负责条线风险管理，并确立了在总分行设立风险管理部门、在部室和支行设置风险管理员的风险管理模式。公司面临的主要风险有信用风险、市场风险、操作风险、流动性风险、声誉风险等，其中又以信用风险、操作风险、流动性风险为主。针对各类风险，公司制定了相应的应对措施，坚持全面风险管理理念，启动全面风险管理系统建设，搭建全面风险管理体系，推行“全员控风险”的风险管理文化，制定了一系列风险管理制度，采取了行之有效的风险管理和控制手段。

## (一) 信用风险管理

公司通过合理配置信贷资源，继续推行法人客户统一授信管理，保证了公司信贷业务平稳健康发展。一是提高风险敏感度，严把评级授信审查关口。面对复杂严峻的市场形势，公司加强对行业、区域风险的研究，不断提高风险敏感度，认真执行“评级、授信、用信”的信贷审查机制，严把授信客户的准入条件，起到了前移信贷风险防范关口的效果。二是严格法人客户的用信审查。各分支机构加强受信企业用信审查，对借款人借款用途、还款来源、经营状况、财务状况进行严格审查，并对第二还款来源进行落实评价，确保用信条件符合公司相关信贷要求。同时按照公司信贷管理相关办法，严格对企业用信时点的相关资产、证件等信贷资料进行收集核实，确保资料真实、有效。三是提升贷后检查实效，严防授信后风险。公司加强贷后跟进管理，坚持以贷款风险分类、用途真实性和风险性检查为主，检查人员深入企业，检查账簿、座谈、核查资产，及时跟踪贷款资金流向，掌握企业的真实经营状况，形成了全面系统的贷后检查报告。

## （二）市场风险管理

公司面临的市场风险主要集中在贷款利率、外汇交易、债券投资等业务。针对上述风险，公司主要采取以下措施：一是严格执行《莱商银行市场风险管理办法》，根据各种业务性质、规模、复杂程度，采用适当的技术方法和手段来识别、计量、监测和控制银行账户中不同类别的市场风险，逐步开发和使用内部模型计量风险价值，并建立和完善市场风险指标体系，使用关键风险指标反映市场风险状况，使经营层能够及时了解市场风险水平，并为市场风险管理提供决策依据。二是在利率波动频繁及票据市场交易不活跃的情况下，公司理性分析宏观政策和市场走势，加强同业协作，保持了较高的盈利水平，并规避了因政策因素导致的利率风险。三是对市场风险实行限额管理，建立并实行了对各类和各级限额的内部审批程序和操作规程，能够根据业务性质、规模、复杂程度和风险承受能力设定并定期审查及时更新。

## (三)操作风险管理

公司持续开展思想道德教育，梳理完善内控制度，加强监督检查工作力度，取得了良好的效果。一是公司开展了制度学习竞赛活动，成立了以行长为组长的制度考核工作小组，活动自2013年4月份起至10月份结束，有效提升了员工执行规章制度和业务流程的主动性、自觉性和实效性。二是完善操作风险管理体系。将对人的管理纳入操作风险管理范畴，在引人、选人、育人和用人方面，从操作风险防控和管理的角度严格把关、科学考评、严厉惩戒。三是建立了常态化员工行为风险排查制度。公司开展了2013年员工思想道德教育活动，通过家访、谈话等形式加强了对员工思想行为排查，形成了风险排查的长效机制，通过加强思想道德体系建设及考核，筑牢了思想防线。

## (四)流动性风险管理

流动性风险管理的目标是通过建立适时、合理、有效的流动性风险管理机制，实现对流动性风险的识别、计量、监测和控制，将流动性风险控制在可以承受的范围之内，以推动公司的持续、稳健运行。一是把握市场利率走势，充分保障公司流动性。公司根据头寸情况与市场行情，适时开展质押式回购，在充分保证流动性的基础上，实现了较好收益。二是加强流动性管理，严防流动性风险。公司持续强化对流动性因素的分析和预测，严格执行资金变化报告制度，持续做好关键时点的资金安排和流动性监测和预算，实时监控资金的流入和流出，随时掌握全行资金使用情况，并合理安排资产负债期限结构匹配，降低资产期限错配风险，确保各项流动性监测指标符合监管要求。三是公司定期开展流动性压力测试，对流动性储备进行分级和精细化管理，提升公司流动性风险应对能力。

## （五）声誉风险管理

公司注重增强声誉风险意识，全面提升对声誉风险认识、应对、处置能力，全年声誉风险管理状况良好。一方面健全完善声誉风险应急机制，公司建立了舆情监测、分析、应对、处置及报告制度，加强舆情关注判断的敏感性，及时发现并处置负面舆情。另一方面加强员工培训，提升全员的危机应对、处理水平。同时，正面引导外部舆情，公司充分利用电视、网络、报刊等媒体，加强正面宣传力度，先后在《莱芜日报》等主流媒体上进行了正面宣传，积极提升社会形象。

## (六) 公司采用的风险评估和计量方法

公司风险计量主要采用定性和定量相结合的方法，由合规风险部会同相关业务部门实施。其中信用风险计量主要采用标准法。市场风险计量方式包括缺口分析、久期分析、外汇敞口分析等。操作风险计量主要采用基本指标法。流动性风险计量主要以流动性比率、指标法为主。公司风险评估和计量方法还不完善，公司将抓紧研究适合莱商银行业务发展的风险计量模型、量化分析方法和风险管理措施，进而从流程、制度、标准、文化、战略等各个方面建立和完善公司的全面风险管理体系。

## 八、内部控制制度的完整性、合理性与有效性

公司继续以防范风险、审慎经营为出发点，本着全面、审慎、有效、独立的原则不断加强内部控制，全面贯彻执行各类内部控制法规，实现了各项业务的快速发展和稳健运行。一是加强合规管理，积极开展合规文化建设，努力培育员工的合规意识，深化全员合规人人有责、合规创造价值等合规理念。切实加强内控制度建设，强化内控制度学习和执行力，提升全员合规操作能力和意识，重点是强化依法合规经营意识，杜绝有章不循、违章操作的行为。在全行范围内形成了主动遵守各项规章制度的良好氛围，有效提升内部控制效力，从源头上防范风险事件的发生。二是建立健全内控制度评价机制。对内部控制制度的建设、执行情况，均由专门人员根据国家法律规定、业务发展状况和市场环境变化等定期进行审计评价，实现制度管理的常态化、持续化和规范化，有效保障内控制度的完整性、合规性和可操作性。三是强化监督检查。由内审部牵头先后对全行业条线的合规性和风险状况进行了24项共计86次检查和审计，覆盖所有分支行；检查发现问题95个，提出整改意见57条；对22个单位主要负责人的岗位交接进行审计。

## VII. Risks of the Company and risk management

The organizational structure for company risk management consists of the Board of Directors and its special committee, senior management and its special committee, the risk management department and the related business department, and risk reporters etc. The Board of Directors is ultimately responsible for risk management. Under the Board of

Directors is a Risk Management Committee, which is responsible for the deliberation and preparation of risk management policies, procedures, quantitative criteria and solutions to major risks of the Company. The Board of Supervisors is responsible for supervising the duty performance of the Board of Directors and senior management in risk management. The senior management is responsible for the specific implementation of risk management policies, under which special committees such as the Credit Review Committee and the Assets-Liabilities Committee have been set up, which is responsible for risk management of business lines; a risk management mode has been established in which a risk management department is set up under the head office and the branches and risk administrators are appointed for departments and sub-branches. Major risks confronting the Company include credit risk, market risk, operational risk, liquidity risk and reputational risk, etc., among which, credit risk, operational risk and liquidity risk are the main risks. The Company has prepared coping measures to address each kind of risks. Sticking to the idea of comprehensive risk management and initiating overall risk management system construction, the Company has established a comprehensive risk management system and implemented the risk management concept of "all staff involved in risk control". A series of risk management systems have been prepared and effective risk management and control measures have been adopted.

##### (i) Management of credit risk

The Company continues to carry out unified credit management of corporate customer through reasonable allocation of credit resources to guarantee stable and sound development of credit operation of the Company. Firstly, risk sensitivity shall be improved and review of credit rating shall be controlled strictly. In the face of complex and severe market situation, the Company strengthens research about industrial and regional risks, continuously improves risk sensitivity, carefully executes "rating, credit and consumption" credit review system and strictly controls access condition of credit customers, achieving the result of moving credit risk prevention pass forward. Secondly, consumption review of corporate customers shall be controlled strictly. All branches shall strengthen consumption review for credit enterprises, strictly review loan purpose, repayment source, operating condition and financial condition of the borrower, evaluate source of the second repayment and guarantee that consumption condition is in accordance with related credit requirements of the Company. At the same time, related assets, certificates and other credit data of consumption time point of the enterprise shall be collected and verified to guarantee data authenticity and validity. Thirdly, post-loan inspection effectiveness shall be improved and post-credit risk shall be prevented strictly. The Company strengthens post-loan follow-up management and focuses on credit risk classification and purpose authenticity and risk inspection. Inspectors go to the enterprise to check account books, have seminars and verify assets, follow loan fund flow promptly, master true operating condition of the enterprise and form comprehensive and systematic post-loan inspection report.

##### (ii) Management of market risk

Market risk confronted by the Company are mainly concentrated on loan interest rate, foreign exchange transaction, bond investment and other businesses. In light of the above risks, the Company mainly takes the following measures: Firstly, Administrative Measures

of Laishang Bank on Market Risk shall be executed strictly and appropriate technical methods and means shall be adopted to identify, measure, monitor and control different types of market risk in the bank account in accordance with nature, scale and complexity of all businesses. In addition, internal model shall be gradually developed and used to measure the value at risk and market risk index system shall be established and improved to reflect the conditions of market risk by key risk indexes and enable management to promptly learn about market risk level and provide decision basis for management of market risk. Secondly, under the situation of frequent fluctuation of interest rate and non-brisk transaction in bill market, the Company rationally analyzes macro policies and market trends and strengthens cooperation in the same trade to maintain high profitability and evade interest rate risk caused by policy factors. Thirdly, market risk are provided with quota management and internal approval procedure and operating procedure for all kinds of and all levels of quotas shall be established, implemented, set, periodically reviewed and promptly updated in accordance with nature, scale, complexity and risk tolerance of all businesses.

##### (iii) Management of operational risk

The Company continues to carry out ideological and moral education, sorts out and improves internal control system, strengthens degree of supervision and inspection and has achieved good result. Firstly, the Company has carried out competition activity in learning system and established system assessment working group with the President as group leader. The activity lasted from April to October 2013, effectively improving initiative, consciousness and effectiveness of employees in executing rules and regulations as well as business process. Secondly, operational risk management system has been improved. Personnel management has been incorporated to scope of operational risk management and strict control, scientific evaluation and severe punishment shall be conducted from the angle of operational risk prevention and control and management in terms of recommending personnel, selecting personnel, cultivating personnel and employing personnel. Thirdly, risk testing system for normal employee behavior has been established. The Company has conducted ideological and moral education activity for employees in 2013 and strengthened testing for employees' thought and behavior through home visiting, conversation and other forms to form long-term mechanism of risk testing, and reinforced defensive line of thought through enhancing construction and assessment of ideological and ethical system.

##### (iv) Management of liquidity risk

Management of liquidity risk aims to identify, measure, monitor and control liquidity risks and control liquidity risks within an acceptable range so as to advance sustainable and stable operation of the Company through establishing appropriate, reasonable and effective liquidity risk management system. Firstly, trend of market interest rate shall be mastered and liquidity of the Company shall be guaranteed adequately. The Company conducts pledge-style repo appropriately in accordance with position and market condition and achieves good benefit on the basis of guaranteeing liquidity adequately. Secondly, liquidity management shall be strengthened and liquidity risk shall be prevented strictly. The Company insists in strengthening analysis and prediction for liquidity factor, strictly

executes capital change report system, continues to conduct capital arrangement of key time point and liquidity monitoring and budget, monitors flow-in and flow-out of capital in real time, grasps capital utilization condition of the whole bank at any time, reasonably arranges matching of asset-liability term structure, reduces risks of assets term mismatch and guarantees that all liquidity monitoring indices are in accordance with supervision requirements. Thirdly, the Company conducts liquidity pressure test periodically and provides hierarchical and delicacy management for liquidity reserve to improve coping capacity of the Company's liquidity risk.

##### (V) Management of reputational risk

The Company pays attention to strengthen awareness of reputational risk and comprehensively improve recognition, coping and disposal capability for reputational risk, with management of reputational risk in good condition throughout the whole year. On the one hand, emergency mechanism for reputational risk shall be strengthened and improved. The Company has established monitoring, analysis, coping, disposal and reporting system for public opinions and strengthens sensitivity for concern and judgment of public opinions to promptly find and dispose of negative public opinions. On the other hand, employee training shall be strengthened and employees' level to cope with and dispose of crisis shall be improved. At the same time, external public opinions shall be guided positively. The Company makes full use of TV, network, newspaper and other media to strengthen positive publicity degree and has successively conducted positive publicity in Laiwu Daily and other mainstream media to positively improve social image.

##### (VI) Risk evaluation and measurement method adopted by the Company

The risk evaluation of the Company mainly adopts qualitative and quantitative methods to be implemented by Compliance Risk Department and related business departments. The measurement of credit risk mainly adopts standardized approach. The measurement of market risk includes gap analysis, duration analysis, foreign exchange exposure analysis, etc. The measurement of operational risk mainly adopts basic indicator approach. The measurement of liquidity risk mainly adopts liquidity ratio and indicator method. The risk evaluation and measurement method of the Company are imperfect and the Company will pay close attention to study risk measurement model, quantitative analysis method and risk management measures fitting business development of Laishang Bank and thus establish and improve comprehensive risk management system of the company with regard to process, system, standard, culture, strategy, etc.

## VIII. Completeness, reasonableness and effectiveness of the internal control system

The Company continuously strengthens internal control, comprehensively implements various internal control regulations and achieves rapid development and stable operation of all businesses with comprehensive, prudent, effective and independent principle on the basis of risk prevention and prudent management. Firstly, compliance management shall be strengthened and compliance cultural construction shall be carried out positively to cultivate compliance awareness of employees and deepen compliance idea of everyone.

being responsible for compliance and compliance creating value of employees. Strengthen internal control system firmly, consolidate the learning of internal control system and execution performance, improve compliance operation capacity and awareness of employees, predominantly strengthen awareness of legal compliance management and eliminate violation and operation against rules. Thus, a good atmosphere where all rules and regulations are followed positively has been formed in the whole bank and internal control effect has been improved effectively to prevent risks event fundamentally. Secondly, evaluation mechanism for internal control system shall be established and improved. Establishment and execution of internal control system shall be audited by evaluated by professionals in accordance with national legal provisions, business development condition, change of market environment to achieve normalization, sustainability and standardization of system management and effectively guarantee completeness, compliance and operability of internal control system. Thirdly, supervision and inspection shall be strengthened. Internal Audit Department has successively taken the lead to check and audit 24 items of compliance and risk condition of business lines of the whole bank for 86 times, covering all branch banks; 95 problems have been found in the inspection and 57 rectification opinions have been put forward; post handovers of principals of 22 departments have been audited.

## （五）董事、监事、高级管理人员薪酬情况

### 1. 薪酬管理架构及决策程序

公司理顺了董事会、监事会、高管层等治理主体在薪酬管理中的职责、重点和任务，建立了科学的薪酬管理架构，明确规定董事、监事的报酬由股东大会决定，董事会决定高级管理人员的报酬。董事会下设提名与薪酬考核委员会，根据公司经营效益情况研究拟订董事会、监事会、高级管理层成员和员工的薪酬水平及构成，包括养老和各种补偿性支付等，提出年度薪酬方案和意见并报董事会审议通过后执行。

### 2. 年度薪酬总量与结构分布情况

2013年，公司薪酬总量为10751万元，薪酬结构分布如下：固定薪酬3225.3万元，占30%，可变薪酬5375.5万元，占50%，福利性收入2150.2万元，占20%。

### 3. 薪酬与业绩衡量、风险调整的标准

公司绩效工资按照《2013年分支机构考核办法》进行考核兑现。公司设立了风险金制度，制订了《风险金管理办法》，办法规定从董事长到员工全员缴纳风险金。风险金在员工离开公司时按审计结果全部或按比例退还。

### 4. 薪酬制度、延期支付和非现金薪酬情况

公司自2010年实施新的薪酬制度---等级行员制，按照“以责定岗、岗位设置精简、专业化”的原则，根据员工岗位职责及标准进行人岗匹配，实行了以岗定薪、按月拨付的固定薪酬。

公司可变薪酬中包含了绩效薪酬和中长期激励约束基金。

自2013年起，公司绩效薪酬发放实行延期支付制度，延期支付时间为3年。其中，高管绩效薪酬的50%实行延期支付，按照兑付50%之后第一年15%，第二年15%，第三年20%的比例分三年兑付。

中长期激励约束基金也实行延期支付，按照当年30%，第二年30%，第三年40%的比例分三期兑付。

##### 公司无非现金薪酬情况。

股东单位出任的董事、监事和独立董事、外部监事2013年度共领取薪酬77万元。

### 6. 年度薪酬方案制定及考核完成情况

公司年度薪酬方案根据人员规模、业务发展情况等制定，2013年度，公司各项经营指标良好，薪酬情况合理的反应了公司业绩及风险管理情况。

7. 超出原定薪酬方案的例外情况

报告期内，公司无超出原定薪酬的例外情况。

#### (v) Remunerations of directors, supervisors and senior management

## 1. Remunerations management structure and decision-making procedure

The Company has rationalized the responsibilities, key points and tasks of governance bodies such as the Board of Directors, Board of Supervisors and senior management in remunerations management. A scientific remunerations management structure has been established, specifying that the remunerations of directors and supervisors is decided by the General Shareholders' Meeting, and the remunerations of senior management is decided by the Board of Directors. Under the Board of Directors is a Nomination and remunerations Assessment Committee, subject to the operational efficiency of the Company, which prepares the remunerations level and remunerations composition of the Board of Directors, Board of Supervisors, members of senior management and employees, including pension and various compensatory payment etc., and puts forward the annual remunerations plan and opinion, to be implemented after being reviewed and approved by the Board of Directors.

## 2. The total amount and structure distribution of remunerations in the year

In 2013, the total amount of remunerations of the Company was RMB 107.51 million and the remunerations structure distribution are as follows: fixed remunerations RMB 32.253 million, accounting for 30%; variable remunerations RMB 53.755 million, accounting for 50%; and welfare income RMB 21.502 million, accounting for 20%.

## 3. Remunerations and the standard of performance measurement and risk adjustment

The Company’s performance salary is assessed and paid according to the 2013 Measures on Assessment of Branches. The Company has established the risk fund system and prepared the Management Measures on Risk Fund, which sets out that all employees from Chairman to ordinary employees shall pay risk fund. The risk fund shall be return in full or pro rata according to auditing results when any employee leaves the Company.

## 4. Salary system, deferred payment and non-cash compensation

The Company has implemented new salary system (grading system) since 2010. According to the principle of “responsibility-based position, simple position setting, specialization”, Person-post matching is conducted based on job responsibilities of employees and standards and fixed salary confirmed by post and paid monthly has been implemented.

Variable salary of the Company includes performance salary and medium and long-term inventive and restrictive fund.

The issuing of performance salary of the Company has been provided with deferred payment system since 2013, with deferred payment time of 3 years. 50% of performance salary of senior management is provided with deferred payment and payment shall be made in three years after paying 50%, with 15% for the first year, 15% for the second year and 20% for the third year.

Medium and long-term inventive and restrictive fund is provided with deferred payment and payment shall be made in three years, with 30% for current year, 30% for the second year and 40% for the third year.

There is no non-cash remuneration in the Company.

5. The annual remuneration of directors, supervisors and senior management are as follows:

6. Preparation of annual remuneration plan and completion of assessment

The Company’s annual remuneration plan is prepared according to the size of staff and business development, etc. In 2013, various operational indicators of the Company were good, and remuneration reasonably reflected the performance and risk management of the company.

7. Exceptions going beyond the original remuneration plan

There were no exceptions beyond the original remuneration plan in the reporting period of the Company.

## (vi) Names of directors, supervisors and senior management who resigned during the reporting period and reasons for resignation

During the reporting period, directors Liu Qingping and independent directors Li Xiulun resigned due to work arrangements.

### II. Employee profile

At the end of the reporting period, there were 1,351 employees in the Company, increasing by 351 from the previous year; among whom employees with a bachelor degree or above amounted to 1,321, accounting for 97.78%.

#### 第五章 公司治理结构

#### 一、公司治理情况

报告期内，公司严格遵守《公司法》和《商业银行法》的有关规定，认真落实监管部门关于公司治理的有关规定，致力于进一步完善公司治理结构，增强公司治理机制的有效性，提高董事会决策的科学性。公司治理具体情况如下：

（一）关于股东与股东大会。公司按照《公司章程》和《股东大会议事规则》的要求召集、召开股东大会，积极听取股东的意见和建议，确保股东对公司重大事项的知情权、参与权和表决权。报告期内，公司召开了2012年度股东大会、2013年第一次临时股东大会、2013年第二次临时股东大会，会议严格按照有关规定履行了相关法律程序，保证了股东参会并行使权利，并由律师出具了法律意见书。

（二）关于董事和董事会。公司现有董事13名，其中独立董事3名。董事会能够严格按照《公司章程》的规定，认真履行职责，在股东大会授权下进行决策，强化公司治理，推进战略步伐，促进稳健经营，维护了公司和全体股东的利益。报告期内公司共召开了4次董事会会议。

目前，公司董事会下设1个办公室和5个专门委员会，包括董事会办公室、关联交易控制委员会、风险管理委员会、战略发展委员会、审计委员会、提名与薪酬考核委员会，其中风险管理委员会下设声誉风险管理专业委员会和信息科技风险管理专业委员会。5个专门委员会具有明确的职权范围，监管公司不同方面的事务，并对董事会负责，为董事会决策提供了建设性建议，进一步提高了董事会决策的科学性。

（三）关于监事和监事会。公司现有监事7人，其中职工监事2人，外部监事2人。监事会按照《公司章程》的规定认真履行职责，对公司财务、风险管理、资产质量以及部分高级管理人员履职的合规性进行监督检查，定期及不定期地与外部审计机构和管理层进行情况交流，有针对性地开展专项检查，针对风险隐患和发现的问题，向董事会和管理层提出建议和改进意见，从而更加有效地发挥监事会的作用。

（四）关于信息披露与透明度。公司在《金融时报》上发布了2012年度报告，并制作了《莱商银行股份有限公司2012年度报告》（摘要）单行本，及时准确地进行信息披露，切实维护广大股东特别是中小投资者的利益。

#### 二、独立董事及履行职责情况

报告期内，公司董事会现有独立董事3人，独立董事本着为全体股东负责的态度，履行诚信和勤勉义务，认真参加董事会及各专门委员会会议，积极发表意见，维护公司整体利益及中小股东

的合法权益不受侵犯。

（一）独立董事参加会议情况。报告期内，独立董事应参加董事会会议15人次，亲自出席14人次，委托出席1人次，缺席0人次。

（二）独立董事对公司有关事项提出异议情况。报告期内，公司独立董事未对公司本年度董事会的议案提出异议。

#### 三、外部监事工作情况

报告期内，公司监事会现有外部监事2人，外部监事本着为全体股东负责的态度，履行诚信和勤勉义务，认真参加监事会及各专门委员会会议，积极发表意见，维护公司整体利益及中小股东的合法权益不受侵犯。

（一）外部监事参加会议情况。报告期内，外部监事应参加监事会会议8人次，亲自出席8人次，委托出席0人次，缺席0人次。

（二）外部监事对公司有关事项提出异议情况。报告期内，公司外部监事未对公司本年度董事会、监事会的议案提出异议。

#### 四、公司经营决策体系

公司最高权力机构是股东大会，通过董事会、监事会进行管理和监督。行长受聘于董事会，对公司日常经营管理全面负责。公司实行一级法人体制，各分支机构均为非独立核算单位，其经营管理活动根据公司授权进行，并对公司负责。

公司无控股股东。公司与大股东在业务、人员、资产、机构、财务五方面完全独立。公司具有独立完整的业务及自主经营能力，董事会、监事会和内部机构能够独立运作。

#### 五、高级管理人员考评及激励机制、制度情况

报告期内，公司董事会进一步完善了对高级管理人员的绩效考核管理办法，建立了薪酬与公司效益和个人业绩相联系的激励约束机制；考核实行百分制，根据各高管人员的工作性质不同，考核项目有所侧重。对于非执行董事、非职工监事的考核主要以其出席会议次数及履职能力考核为主。制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会董事履职评价制度》，主要分为客观评价、自我评价和相互评价三个部分，根据各项指标具体内容的不同，给予不同的比重。2012年，公司制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会对高管人员履职评价办法》、《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对董事、监事、高管人员履职评价办法》、《关于追究董事、监事及高管人员责任的规定》。同时，公司监事会进一步量化考核标准，制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对高管人员履职评价办法实施细则》，明确了监事会对高级管理人员的履职评价，加大了履职问责力度。

# Chapter V Corporate Governance Structure

## I. Corporate governance of the Company

During the reporting period, the Company has strictly observed the related provisions in the Company Law and the Law on Commercial Banks, conscientiously implemented the related regulations of the regulatory intuition on corporate governance, and been dedicated to further improving corporate governance structure, enhancing the effectiveness of the corporate governance mechanism and making the decision of the Board of Directors more scientific. The performance of the Company in its corporate governance is as follows:

##### (i) Shareholders and shareholders' general meeting.

The Company has convened the shareholders’ general meeting in accordance with the requirement of the Articles of Association and the Rules of Procedures for Shareholders’ Meeting and actively listened to shareholders’ opinion and suggestion to ensure that all shareholders have the right to know, to participate and to vote to the major events of the company. During the reporting period, the Company held the 2012 annual shareholders’ general meeting, 2013 first interim shareholders’ general meeting, and 2013 second interim shareholders’ general meeting. The meetings went through related legal procedure in strict accordance with related regulations to ensure that shareholders participate in the meetings and exercise their right. Also, legal opinion was issued by a lawyer.

#### (ii) Directors and the Board of Directors.

Currently, the Company has 13 directors, including 3 independent directors. The Board of Directors is able to conscientiously perform their duties in strict accordance with the provisions in the Articles of Association, makes decisions with the authorization of the shareholders' general meeting, strengthens corporate governance, advances strategic steps and promotes steady operation which safeguarded the interests of the Company and all shareholders. During the reporting period, the Company held altogether 4 board meetings. Currently, under the Board of Directors of the Company, there is 1 office and 5 special committees, including the office of the Board of Directors, related transaction control committee, risk management committee, strategic development committee, audit committee, and nomination and remuneration assessment committee. Under the risk management committee are the reputation risk management special committee and the information technology risk management special committee. The 5 special committees have specific scopes of power, are accountable to the Board of Directors. They provide constructive suggestions for the decision making of the Board of Directors in a timely manner to improve the science of the decisions make by the Board of Directors.

#### (iii) Supervisors and the Board of Supervisors.

The Company has 7 supervisors, including 2 employee supervisors and 2 external supervisors. The Board of Supervisors conscientiously performs its duties in strict accordance with the provisions in the Articles of Association, carries out supervision and inspection to the compliance.

of the company finance, risk management, assets quality and the duty performance of the senior management, communicates with the external audit units and the management regularly and irregularly, carries out targeted special inspection, and puts forward suggestions and improvement opinions to the Board of Directors and the management according to risks and problems discovered, so as to play the role of the Board of Supervisors more effectively.

##### (iv) Information disclosure and transparency.

The Company has published its 2012 Annual Report on Financial Times, and prepared a separate edition of 2012 Annual Report of Laishang Co., Ltd. (summary), to carry out information disclosure in a timely and accurate manner and safeguard the interests of shareholders, particularly medium and small shareholders.

## II. Independent directors and their performance of duties

During the reporting period, there were 3 independent directors on the Board of Directors. With an attitude of being responsible to all shareholders, independent directors performed the obligation of integrity and due diligence, earnestly participate in board meetings and meetings of various special committees and actively delivered their opinions to safeguard the collective interest of the Company and the legitimate rights and interest of medium and small shareholders.

#### (i) Independent directors' participation in meetings.

During the reporting period, the number of independent directors attending the board meetings shall be 15, with presence in 14 persons, entrusted attendance in 1 person, and absence in 0 person.

### (ii) Objections of the independent directors to related affairs of the Company.

During the reporting period, the independent directors of the Company did not object to any proposal of the Board of Directors of the year.

## III. Work of the External Supervisor

There are 2 external supervisors in the Board of Supervisors in the Company during the report period. The External Supervisor shall be responsible to all shareholders, perform duty of integrity and diligence, carefully participate in the meeting of the Board of Supervisors and all special committee meetings, positively give opinions and prevent overall benefit of the Company and lawful rights and interests of medium and small shareholders from infringement.

##### (i) External Supervisors' participation in meeting.

During the report, the number of External Supervisors attending the Board of Supervisors Meeting shall be 8, with presence in 8 persons, entrusted attendance in 0 person and absence in 0 person.

#### (ii) Objection raised by the External Supervisor.

During the report, the External Supervisors of the Company fail to raise objections to the proposals of the Board of Shareholders and Board of Supervisors of the Company for the current year.

## IV. Operation and decision-making system of the Company

The highest authority of the Company is the Shareholders' General Meeting, which is managed and supervised through the Board of Directors and the Board of Supervisors. The President, employed by the Board of Directors, is responsible for all daily operation and management of the Company. Only the head office of the Company is its legal person, and all subsidiaries are non-independent accounting units, whose operation and management activities shall be carried out with the authorization of the Company and who shall be responsible for the Company.

The Company has no controlling shareholder. The Company and its large shareholders are completely independent in terms of business, personnel, assets, organizations and finance. The Company has independent and complete business and independent operation ability, and the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors and internal organizations can operate independently.

## V. Assessment and incentive mechanism and system of senior management

During the reporting period, the Board of Directors of the Company has further improved the method of performance assessment and management of its senior management, and established an incentive and constraint mechanism in which remunerations are associated with the performance of the Company and personal performance; the centesimal system has been carried out for assessment subject to the working nature of senior management and different management positions have different key points for assessment. The assessment of non-executive directors and non-employee supervisors has mainly focused on their attendance on meetings and ability to perform their duties. The Evaluation System of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Duty Performance of Directors on the Board of Directors, which includes such three parts as objective evaluation, self-evaluation and mutual evaluation, has been prepared. Different proportions are given according to different contents of various indexes. In 2012, the Measures of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Assessment of Senior Management's Performance, the Measures of the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Assessment of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Management's Performance and the Provisions of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Accountability of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Management have been formulated. Meanwhile, the Board of Supervisors quantified the criteria of assessment, formulated the Implementing Rules of Duty Performance Assessment Method on Senior Management by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., specified the duty performance assessment on senior management by the Board of Supervisors and strengthened the accountability of duty performance.

### 第六章 股东大会情况

报告期内公司共召开了三次股东大会，具体情况如下：

2013年3月12日，在公司综合营业楼一楼礼堂召开了2012年度股东大会，出席会议的股东及股东代理人共88名，代表股份91596.0527万股，占公司总股本的91.6%。大会审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会2012年度工作报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会2012年度工作报告》、《关于调整经营层目标考核口径的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2012年度审计报告》、《关于审议<莱商银行股份有限公司2012年度财务报告及利润分配方案>的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司三年发展规划（2013-2015）》、《莱商银行股份有限公司三年资本补充计划与拨备提取计划（2013-2015）》、《关于审议<莱商银行股份有限公司2013年机构发展计划>的议案》、《关于对<莱商银行股份有限公司2012年度报告>（摘要）进行信息披露的议案》、《关于审议<莱商银行股份有限公司资本公积金转增股本方案>的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2012年度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况的报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2012年度风险管理报告》。

2013年8月22日，在公司综合营业楼六楼会议室召开了2013年第一次临时股东大会，出席会议的股东及股东代理人共80名，代表股份91349.1260万股，占公司总股本的91.35%。大会审议通过了《关于审议莱商银行股份有限公司第三届董事会董事刘庆平辞职申请的议案》、《关于购买莱商银行临沂分行营业场所的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对董事2012年度履职评价报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对高级管理人员2012年度履职评价报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对监事2012年度履职评价报告》、《关于审议莱商银行股份有限公司第三届董事会独立董事李秀仑辞职申请的议案》、《关于更改莱商银行济南分行营业办公楼购置意向的议案》、《关于购买莱商银行聊城分行营业场所的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司三年资本规划（2013-2015）》、《关于对莱芜钢铁集团有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》、《关于对新汶矿业集团有限责任公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》、《关于对山东泰山钢铁集团有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》、《关于对泰丰纺织集团有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年上半年内部人和股东关联交易执行情况的报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年上半年风险管理报告》。

2013年12月4日，在公司综合营业楼六楼会议室召开了2013年第二次临时股东大会，出席会议的股东及股东代理人共79名，代表股份91232.8727万股，占公司总股本的91.23%。大会审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司股权管理办法》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年1-9月份内部人和股东关联交易执行情况的报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年1-9月份风险管理报告》、

《关于变更莱商银行股份有限公司注册资本的议案》、《关于修改<莱商银行股份有限公司章程>的议案》、《关于发行20亿元人民币涉农金融债券的议案》、《关于调整2013年度目标计划的议案》、《莱商银行2014年机构发展计划》、《关于建立补充医疗保险制度的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司外部监事工作制度》。

### Chapter VI Shareholders' General Meeting

Three shareholders’ general meetings of the Company have been held during the reporting period. Details are as follows:

On March 12, 2013, the 2012 Shareholders' General Meeting was held in the auditorium on the first floor of the comprehensive business building of the Company. 88 shareholders and their authorized agents attended the meeting, representing 915,960,527 shares, accounting for 91.6% of the total Company share capital. The meeting considered and passed 2012 Annual Work Report of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2012 Annual Work Report of the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Adjusting Objective Assessment Criterion of Management, 2012 Annual Audit Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Reviewing '2012 Annual Financial Report and Profit Distribution Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.' Three-Year (2013-2015) Development Planning of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Three-Year (2013-2015) Capital Supplement Scheme and Provision Withdrawing Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Reviewing '2013 Organizational Development Planning of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.' Proposal on Information Disclosure in '2012 Annual Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd(summary).' Proposal on Reviewing 'Scheme of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. about Transferring Capital Reserves to Capital Stock', Report on Executing Related Transactions by Insiders and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. in 2012, and 2012 Risk Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.

On August 22, 2013, the First Interim Shareholders' General Meeting was held in the meeting room on the sixth floor of the comprehensive business building of the Company. 80 shareholders and their authorized agents attended the meeting, representing 913,491,260 shares, accounting for 91.35% of the total Company share capital. The meeting considered and passed Proposal on Reviewing 'Application for Resignation by Liu Qingping, the Director of the 3rd Session of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.' Proposal of Purchasing the Office Building of LinYi Branch of Laishang Bank, Evaluation Report on 2012 Annual Duty Performance of Directors by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Evaluation Report on 2012 Annual Duty Performance of Senior Management by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Evaluation Report on 2012 Annual Duty Performance of Supervisors by the Board of Supervisors.

of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Reviewing ‘Application for Resignation by Li Xiulun, the Independent Director of the 3rd Session of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.’ Proposal on Changing the Intention to Purchase the Office Building of Jinan Branch of Laishang Bank, Proposal on Purchasing Business premises of Liaocheng Branch of Laishang Bank, Three-Year (2013-2015) Capital Planning of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Comprehensive Credit Extension for Laiwu Steel Group Co., Ltd. and Its Affiliated Enterprises, Proposal on Comprehensive Credit Extension for Xinwen Mining Group Co., Ltd. and Its Affiliated Enterprises, Proposal on Comprehensive Credit Extension for Shandong Taishan Steel Group Co., Ltd. and Its Affiliated Enterprises, Proposal on Comprehensive Credit Extension for Taifeng Textile Group Co., Ltd. and Its Affiliated Enterprises, Report on Executing Related Transactions by Insiders and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. in the first half year of 2013 and Report on Risk Management of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. in the first half year of 2013.

On December 4, 2013, the Second Interim Shareholders' General Meeting was held in the meeting room on the sixth floor of the comprehensive business building of the Company. 79 shareholders and their authorized agents attended the meeting, representing 912,328,727 shares, accounting for 91.23% of the total Company share capital. The meeting considered and passed Administrative Measures for Stock Equity of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Report on Executing Related Transactions by Insiders and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. from January to September, 2013, Report on Risk Management of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. from January to September, 2013, Proposal on Changing Registered Capital of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Modifying Articles of Association of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Issuing RMB 2 Billion Agriculture-related Financial Bond, Proposal on Adjusting 2013 Annual Target Plan, Institutional Development Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. in 2014, Proposal on Establishing Supplementary Medical Insurance System and Working System of External Supervisor of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.

## 第七章 董事会报告

## 一、2013年度主要经营管理情况

## （一）稳健经营科学发展，综合实力进一步提升。

资产总额512.82亿元，较年初增加85.38亿元，增长19.98%；各项存款余额349.85亿元，较年初增加53.81亿元，增长18.18%；各项贷款余额251.57亿元，较年初增加46.05亿元；不良贷款余额30977万元，较年初增加21742万元，不良率1.23%，上升0.77个百分点；实现净利润5.90亿元；缴纳各种税金4.32亿元；资本充足率15.9%，较上年下降3.23个百分点；资产利润率1.25%；资本利润率11.35%；成本收入比31.91%；拨备覆盖率273.26%。2013年，公司被莱芜市委市政府授予“2013年度银行业金融机构发展贡献奖”；被中国社科院金融研究所授予2012年度最佳公司治理城市商业银行，被《金融时报》授予“年度最佳社会责任中小银行”；在英国《银行家》杂志评选出的全球前1000家银行中，莱商银行排名第725位。

## （二）充分发挥战略决策作用，科学把握发展方向。

2013年，董事会及其战略发展委员会坚持科学发展和积极稳妥的总基调，推进落实公司发展战略实施，各项工作取得新成效。一是稳步推进机构建设步伐。根据年初制定的机构发展计划，年内完成了济宁分行、泰安分行、临沂分行、聊城分行的开业，莱芜牛泉支行、菏泽东明支行、徐州丰县支行、济南历下支行、济南槐荫支行顺利开业。异地分支机构的稳步发展，在延伸服务半径的同时，调整公司的业务结构，并有效促进了公司规模效益的提升。二是有序推进村镇银行建设发展。2013年4月10日，本公司发起设立的天津武清村镇银行正式挂牌营业，与其挂钩的山西忻州五台莱商村镇银行也取得了筹建批复，目前各项开业前的准备工作正在有序进行中。东营莱商村镇银行和河南方城凤裕村镇银行也呈现出良好的发展态势，各项业务稳步推进，机构布局范围不断扩大，服务半径不断延伸。三是逐步完善战略管理体系。2013年，董事会及其战略发展委员会对实施完成的《三年发展规划（2010-2012）》进行了评估，认为战略目标、业务目标、工作重点等已经基本完成，规划是客观的、合理的、有效的。在制定了总体发展规划后，又陆续制定了资本、机构发展、科技等专项发展规划，形成了总体与单项、长期与短期相结合的战略管理体系，同时公司根据监管要求，对未来经济形势和监管政策进行进一步分析，着手制定战略转型规划，以适应未来的发展。

## （三）加强资本管理，夯实业务发展基础。

2013年，董事会高度重视资本管理工作，从完善制度规划、夯实资本基础、推动转变发展方式等多个方面，较好的发挥了资本在经营管理中的推动作用。一是完善资本管理规划。根据监管要求，修订完善了《莱商银行资本管理规划（2013-2015）》，《莱商银行资本充足率达标计划（2013-2018）》，对未来几年的业务发展、信贷扩张、盈利能力进行了测算，明确过渡期内的资本充足率达标安排，并对资本管理提出了新的要求，保证资本充足指标持续达标。二是顺利完成资本公积金转增股本工作。根据年初董事会制定的《资本公积金转增股本方案》，按照每10股转增10股的要求，2013年第二次临时股东大会审议了变更注册资本和修改公司章程的议案，公司于12月31日在取得监管部门同意变更注册资本的文件后，完成了工商登记的变更，公司注册资本达到20亿元。三是推动向资本集约型发展方式转变。公司注重内部资本积累和外源性资本补充的合理平衡，积极优化资本结构，大力发展资本占用低的小微业务和个人业务，提升资本使用效率。公司成立了推动实施新资本协议领导小组，并在小组下设经济资本考核小组、风险计量与评估小组、科技系统建设小组、业务创新小组。董事会要求经营层在考核时引入经济增加值和风险调整后的收益率2个指标，充分考虑了资本占用、风险成本等因素，通过资本约束严格控制风险资产的扩张，引导公司向资本集约型发展方式转变。

## （四）推动改革创新和转型发展，增强核心竞争力

2013年，董事会深入研究形势变化和监管要求，坚持以改革创新促进转型发展，推动完善经营管理体制机制，不断增强公司的核心竞争力。一是扎实推进组织架构优化。在董事会的指导下，按照“精简效能”的原则对原有组织架构进行了优化，进一步完善了总行部室设置，明确职责划分，提高工作效率，完善“总—分—支”三级行管理模式，各种流程和考核体系更加优化，经营管理体制转型取得新进展。二是积极服务实体经济。持续优化信贷结构，合理配置信贷资源。坚持服务中小企业的市场定位不动摇，董事会要求经营层将信贷资金主要投向中小企业，并结合中小企业资金需求“短、频、快”的特点，进一步细分客户，为中小企业做好金融服务。同时利用好20亿元小微企业金融债，全部用于支持授信500万元以下的小微企业。继续推进主办行制度和信用贷款业务，在第一批信用客户成功推出的基础上，最终选定36户贷款客户为第二批信用贷款客户。为及时总结经验，对第一批信用客户进行了调研，及时退出不符合条件的信用客户，有效保护信贷资产的安全。三是全方位提升客户服务水平。按照董事会提出的“服务无止境、服务无小事、服务精细化”原则，指导经营层不断完善服务渠道和服务手段，努力服务于各层面客户，丰富完善产品功能。完成了金融IC卡的发卡，推进了手机银行、金凤智付通、“即时贷”产品、全国柜面通、公务卡等项目，满足客户的需求。同时继续加快渠道建设，通过提升客服中心、加大自助设备布放、完善跨平台系统功能等方式，增强渠道服务水平。

## （五）强化风险管控，保障业务健康发展

2013年，董事会积极履行风险管理的最终职责，密切关注市场变化，认真分析经营发展中的各类风险，不断完善风险管理体系，保障公司稳健运营。一是提升信用风险管理敏感度。按照监管要求，围绕“客户、产品、行业、区域”实行主动风险管理，强化政策的导向与管控作用，针对中小企业经营特点和风险特征确定风险识别与管理方法，制定符合公司要求的授信管理办法，规范并加强授信风险管理，有效促进公司信贷业务的健康发展。公司与农行莱芜分行、市农村信用联社在充分调研的基础上，协商建立了“存量客户信贷合作协商机制”，在信贷客户维护、信用风险预警、问题联动处置等方面进行深入的合作，进一步提高银银合作水平，对共同抵御信贷风险，促进莱芜地区实体经济的发展起到了积极作用。二是强化关联交易管理。董事会进一步强化对关联交易的管理，积极完善关联交易管理机制，优化关联交易的审批流程，对莱芜钢铁集团有限公司、新汶矿业集团有限责任公司、山东泰山钢铁集团有限公司、泰丰纺织集团有限公司及其关联企业进行了综合授信。经营层按季度向董事会报告关联交易情况，对关联交易的公允性和风险进行充分的讨论和审查，强化公司对关联交易风险的管理和控制。三是提升信息科技风险管控水平。2013年，董事会信息科技风险专业委员会要求经营层继续完善信息科技风险的管控，聘请安永咨询公司合作建立莱商银行信息科技风险管理体系，并完善了《莱商银行业务连续性管理办法》等10余项制度。同时加强业务连续性管理，以业务持续为目标，建立应对突发事件、灾难灾害的协同管理体系，进一步加强整体业务连续性规范和深层次机制建设，实现对事故和灾难的有效应对，推动公司持续、稳健发展。四是指导经营层有效化解与核销不良贷款。2013年，公司面对严峻复杂的经济形势和下行压力，为了有效防止不良和拨备“两高”局面的出现，加大了不良贷款的核销力度，同时做到帐销案存权在。对出现的其他不良贷款进行了严格的责任认定和细分，真实反映贷款形态，并积极化解和清收。

## （六）积极履行社会责任，彰显企业社会价值。

2013年，董事会继续积极践行社会责任，加强企业文化建设，推动公司建立社会责任长效机制，展现公司良好的社会形象。一是全力维护利益相关者权益。充分尊重和公平对待全体股东，努力为股东创造良好的投资回报。坚持合规、诚信经营，致力于不断提升客户服务体验，保障存款人的合法权益。二是引领企业坚定发展信心，银企携手共度难关。坚持“融资与融智”结合的支持理念，以“存在·规律·掌控”为主题举办中小企业发展论坛第六次会议，政银企共同讨论危中求机的发展策略，邀请四省十一市的600余户中小企业主要负责人参加，特聘工信部专家授课并现场解答企业提出的问题，在各地企业家中引起强烈的反响。此外，公司坚持“能拉不推”，对于暂时性经营困难的企业，主动帮助企业，实行优惠利率，降低企业财务成本，帮助一大批企业度过了难关。三是稳步推进企业文化建设。在机构改革时增设了企业文化部，并聘请专业公司完成了企业文化体系的梳理工作，初步完成了包括核心价值观、使命与愿景等在内的企业文化核心理念整理工作，制作了新版的企业形象宣传片，对包括行徽、行名、户外广告等视觉识别系统元素进行了重新的设计。四是继续热心公益事业。2013年，公司继续参加慈心一日捐、春蕾计

划、义务献血、冠名山东女排、参加全市创城志愿者活动，捐款200万元设立了“慈母基金”，为芦山地震灾区捐款100万元，社会知名度和公信力有效提升，被授予“山东省履行社会责任示范企业”荣誉称号。

## 三、董事会日常工作情况

##### 报告期内公司董事会共召开了4次会议，具体情况如下：

（一）2013年1月22日，召开第三届董事会第七次会议，审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会2012年度工作报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2012年度经营工作报告》、《关于调整经营层目标考核口径的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2012年度审计报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2012年度财务报告及利润分配方案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司三年发展规划（2013-2015）》、《莱商银行股份有限公司三年资本补充计划与拨备提取计划（2013-2015）》、《莱商银行股份有限公司资本充足率达标规划（2013-2018）》、《关于实施组织架构优化方案的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年机构发展计划的议案》、《关于审议<莱商银行股份有限公司资本公积金转增股本方案>的议案》、《关于对〈莱商银行股份有限公司2012年度报告〉进行信息披露的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2012年度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况的报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2012年度风险管理报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司关于召开2012年度股东大会的议案》。

（二）2013年5月3日，召开第三届董事会第八次会议，审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年1季度经营工作报告》、《关于对莱商银行股份有限公司经营管理层授权的议案》、《关于审议董事刘庆平辞职申请的议案》、《关于购买临沂分行办公楼的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会对董事2012年度履职评价报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会对高级管理人员2012年度履职评价报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年1季度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况的报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年1季度风险管理报告》、《关于对个别不良贷款进行核销的议案》、听取第三届董事会风险管理委员会关于业务连续性的报告和第三届董事会科技风险管理专业委员会关于信息科技工作的报告。

（三）2013年7月26日，召开第三届董事会第九次会议，审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年上半年经营工作报告》、《关于审议独立董事李秀仑辞职申请的议案》、《关于更改济南分行营业办公楼购置意向的议案》、《关于购买聊城分行营业场所的议案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司三年资本规划（2013-2015）》、《关于对莱芜钢铁集团有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》、《关于对新汶矿业集团有限责任公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》、《关于对山东泰山钢铁集团有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》、《关于对泰丰纺织集团有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》、《莱商银行组织架构方案》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年上半年内部人和股东关联交易执行情况的报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年上半年风险管理报告》、《关于召开2013年第一次临时股东大会的议案》。

（四）2013年11月15日，召开第三届董事会第十次会议，审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年1-9月份经营工作报告》、《第三届董事会对<莱商银行股份有限公司三年发展规划

（2010–2012）>的评估报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司股权管理办法》、《莱商银行风险管理办法》、《莱商银行操作风险管理办法》、《莱商银行流动性风险管理办法》、《莱商银行市场风险管理办法》、《莱商银行合规风险管理办法》、《莱商银行国别风险管理办法》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年1–9月份内部人和股东关联交易执行情况的报告》、《莱商银行股份有限公司2013年1–9月份风险管理报告》、《关于聘任杨宏峰为莱商银行副行长的议案》、《关于变更莱商银行股份有限公司注册资本的议案》、《关于修改<莱商银行股份有限公司章程>的议案》、《关于调整专门委员会人员组成的议案》、《关于发行20亿元人民币涉农金融债券的议案》、《关于调整2013年度目标计划的议案》、《关于在经营层聘任总行级巡视员（顾问）的议案》、《莱商银行2014年机构发展计划》、《关于核销莱芜市本先工贸有限公司不良贷款的议案》、《关于核销莱芜市东源贸易有限公司不良贷款的议案》、《关于成立上市工作领导小组和上市工作办公室的议案》、《关于建立补充医疗保险制度的议案》、《关于召开2013年第二次临时股东大会的议案》；通报了《关于莱商银行长效约束与激励实施情况的报告》。

## 四、2014年工作计划

## (一) 工作思路：

按照监管要求和董事会的战略部署，继续坚持积极稳妥的发展战略，实现理财业务、国际业务、个人业务与同业合作的新突破；坚持“三个服务”的市场定位不动摇，下沉业务重心，深耕细作，区别对待，实现差异化、特色化、市场化的发展转型；落实全面风险管理，努力盘活和化解不良资产，切实防范声誉风险和流动性风险，保持恰当的存贷比例，严防各类案件的发生；加强资本管理，重视发展低资本消耗的业务产品，严格控制风险资产的增加，完善以经济资本管理为导向的考核体系建设；强化人力资源管理和机构管理，优化资源配置，实现机构、市场、产品、业务、人才等发展要素的和谐匹配，确保完成全年各项目标任务，保持持续发展能力。

## （二）工作目标：

1. 确保资本充足率保持在12%以上，其中核心一级资本充足率保持在10%以上；

2. 年存款增长100亿元，存贷比控制在75%以内；

3. 实现拨备前利润（税前）11亿元，净资产利润率保持在12%以上，成本收入比控制在35%以内，中间业务收入占比6%以上；

4. 不良贷款率控制在3%以内，贷款损失准备充足率在280%以上，拨贷比达到2.5%以上；

5. 风险监管评级保持在二类行标准；

6. 实现安全营运全年无道德案件和重大责任事故发生。

## （三）工作重点

1. 加强自身建设，提高决策能力。针对利率市场化和存款保险制度等对中小银行的冲击，借助战略投资者上海浦东发展银行平台，加强战略管理。利用董事会换届工作，进一步提高各专门委员会专业能力，通过调研分析，形成专项报告，为董事会决策提供充分可行依据，提升董事会战略决策职能，提高董事会的决策水平。

2. 深化改革创新和转型发展。坚持“三个服务”的市场定位，围绕监管要求，详细分析国内宏观经济形势和法律法规的要求，以实现持续稳健发展为目标，创新完善营销和服务模式，建立人力资源管理的长期发展规划，加强绩效管理，健全激励有效、约束到位的考核机制和合理有效的资源配置机制，提高资源配置效率和整体收益水平，在确立的市场定位下实现机构、产品、人员、规模、考核体系的协调发展。

3. 强化资本管理，拓展资本补充渠道。按照制定的资本管理规划，做好内部资本充足的评估，指导经营层建立健全资本管理的制度和流程，探索资本管理新机制，增强资本约束下的持续经营能力，强化经济资本对转型发展的引导作用。同时公司上市工作领导小组启动上市工作程序，并积极探索优先股、创新二级资本工具等渠道，实现多渠道补充资本。

4. 以案件防控为主线，加强全面风险管理。进一步加强完善思想道德体系建设，重点防控流动性风险和声誉风险，以及关联交易、行业集中度和单一客户贷款集中度和单一集团客户授信集中度等过高带来的风险，启动全面风险管理系统建设，严格大额贷款授信管理，进一步完善不良贷款“防控化”机制建设，加强不良资产的清收和转化，成立“责任认定追究委员会”。

5. 提高综合服务能力。不断深化服务理念，完善分层面、多功能、多渠道服务客户的方式方法，加强客户细分，增强科技支撑力、产品竞争力和营销服务能力。同时探索网点转型，创新社区和小微支行发展的模式，以小微业务促进管理和服务提升，形成完善的管理、营销、服务、风控、盈利模式的转型，以国际业务和理财业务为抓手，大力发展中间业务，提高公司的综合实力和盈利能力。

## 五、利润分配方案

按照10%的比例计提法定盈余公积，按2%的比例计提任意盈余公积，每股按0.05元（含税）进行分配，按规定提取长期激励计划基金。上述分配方案执行后，结余未分配利润作为企业积累转作下年使用。

### Chapter VII Report of the Board of Directors

## I. Review of main operation and management in 2013

## (i) Steady operation and scientific development and further upgrading of comprehensive strength

The total assets reached RMB 51.28 billion, increasing by RMB 8.538 billion as compared to the beginning of the year, representing a growth rate of 19.98%; the balance of various deposits was RMB 34.985 billion, increasing by RMB 5.381 billion as compared to the beginning of the year, representing a growth rate of 18.18%; the balance of various loans was RMB 25.157 billion, increasing by RMB 4.605 billion as compared to the beginning of the year; the balance of non-performing loans was RMB 309.77 million, increasing by RMB 217.42 million as compared to the beginning of the year, and the non-performing rate was 1.23%, increasing by 0.77 percentage point; the net profit realized was RMB 590 million; various taxes paid reached RMB 432 million; the capital adequacy ratio was 15.9%, decreasing by 3.23 percentage point as compared with the previous year; the asset profit ratio assets was 1.25%; the rate of return on capital was 11.35%; the cost-to-income ratio was 31.91%; and the provision coverage was 273.26%. In 2013, the Company was awarded with the title of “Contribution Award of 2013 Banking Financial Institutions Development” by Municipal Party Committee and Government of Laiwu City, and the title of “the City Commercial Bank with the Best Corporate Governance in 2012” by the Financial Research Institute of the Chinese Academy of Social Sciences; the Company was also awarded with the title of “Annual Medium and Small Banks with the Best Social Responsibility” by Financial Times, and ranked No. 725 among the top 1,000 banks in the world issued by British magazine the Bankers.

## (ii) Bring strategic decision role into full play and grasp development direction scientifically

Board of Shareholders and Strategic Development Committee adhered to the overall keynote of scientific development and prudence, advanced implementation of development strategy of the Company and all work made new achievements in 2013. Firstly, agency construction was advanced stably. Jining branch, Tai'an branch, Linyi branch, Liaocheng branch, Laiwu Niuquan sub-branch, Heze Dongming sub-branch, Xuzhou Fengxian sub-branch, Jinan Lixia sub-branch and Jinan Huaiyin sub-branch were established smoothly in the year in accordance with agency development plan formulated at the beginning of the year. Stable development of nonlocal branches has extended service radius, adjusted the business structure and effectively improved scale benefit of the Company. Secondly, construction and development of county banks were advanced in order. Tianjin Wuqing County Bank launched and established by the Company formally operated on April 10, 2013 and associated Laishang County Bank of Shanxi Xinzhou Wutai obtained reply to construction. At present, preparatory work prior to opening is conducted in order. Dongying Laishang County Bank and Henan Fangcheng Fengyu County Bank developed well and all businesses were advanced stably. In addition, the agency layout scope and service radius were extended continuously. Thirdly,

strategic management system was improved gradually. The Board of Shareholders and Strategic Development Committee evaluated Three-Year (2010-2012) Development Planning completed in 2013 and strategic objective, business objective and work emphasis have been completed basically. The planning was objective, reasonable and effective. Assets, agency development, technology and other special development planning were formulated successively after formulating overall development planning to form overall and single and long-term and short-term strategic management system. At the same time, the Company further analyzed economic situation and supervision policy in the future in accordance with supervision requirements and focused on formulating strategic transformation planning to adapt to future development.

## (iii) Strengthen capital management and consolidate business development foundation

The Board of Directors attached great importance to capital management in 2013 and the impetus role of capital in operation and management from several aspects including improving system planning, consolidating capital foundation and advancing transformation of development mode. Firstly, capital management planning was improved. The Capital Management Planning of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.(2013-2015) and the Attainment Plan for Capital Adequacy Ratio of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. (2013-2018) were revised and improved in accordance with supervision requirements and business development, credit expansion and profitability in next few years were calculated to define attainment arrangement of capital adequacy ratio in transition period and new requirements were proposed for capital management to guarantee that capital adequacy index reaches standard continuously. Secondly, capital reserves were transferred to capital stock. The 2nd Extraordinary General Meeting in 2013 reviewed the proposal of changing registered capital and modifying Articles of Association as per 10 shares transferred to 10 shares in accordance with Scheme of Transferring Capital Reserves to Capital Stock formulated by the Board of Directors at the beginning of the year. The Company completed change of industrial and commercial registration after obtaining documents of supervision department agreeing to change registered capital on December 31 and the registered capital of the Company reached RMB 2 billion. Thirdly, transformation to capital-intensive development mode was advanced. The Company paid high attention to reasonable balance between internal capital accumulation and extrinsic capital supplement, positively optimized capital structure, vigorously developed small and micro business and individual business occupying low capital and improved capital utilization efficiency. The Company established a leading group boosting implementation of new capital agreement and the group included Economic Capital Assessment Group, Risk Measurement and Evaluation Group, Technology System Construction Group and Business Innovation Group. The Board of Directors required the management to introduce two indices, economic value added and rate of return after risk adjustment at the time of assessment and adequately considered capital occupation, risk cost, etc., strictly controlled expansion of risk assets through capital constraint and guided the Company to transform towards capital-intensive development mode.

## (iv) Advance reform and innovation and transformation development and strengthen core competitiveness

In 2013, the Board of Directors profoundly studied situation change and supervision requirement, insisted in promoting transformation development with reform and innovation, boosted

improvement of operation and management system mechanism and continuously reinforced core competitiveness of the Company. Firstly, organizational structure was optimized solidly. Under guidance of the Board of Directors, original organizational structure was optimized with principle of "simplifying efficiency" and setting of department of the corporation was further improved to clarify separation of duties, improve working efficiency. In addition, "corporation-branch-subsidiary" three-level management mode was improved and all procedures and assessment systems were optimized, with transformation of operation and management system making new progress. Secondly, real economy was served vigorously. Credit structure shall be optimized continuously and credit resources shall be allocated reasonably. Adhering to market positioning of serving small and medium-sized enterprises, the Board of Directors required the management to input credit fund mainly to small and medium-sized enterprises and further subdivide customers in accordance with "short, frequent and fast" capital demand of small and medium-sized enterprises to provide financial services for small and medium-sized enterprises. At the same time, financial bond of small and micro enterprises of RMB 2 billion shall be used to support small and micro enterprises with credit below RMB 5 million. Leading bank system and credit loan business shall be further boosted. With the first batch of credit customers introduced successfully, 36 loan customers were finally selected as the second batch of credit customers. To summarize experience promptly, the first batch of credit customers were investigated to promptly eliminate credit customers not complying with conditions and effectively protect safety of credit assets. Thirdly, customer service level was improved comprehensively. The management was guided to continuously improve service channel and service means, endeavor to serve customers of all levels and richen and improve production function in accordance with principle "serve endlessly, serve trivially and serve elaborately" proposed by the Board of Directors. Financial IC cards were issued and mobile banking, Jinfengzhifutong, "instant loan" product, national all-in-one counter, business card and other projects were boosted to satisfy customer demand. Channel building-up shall be accelerated and channel service level shall be reinforced through improving customer service center, increasing layout of self-service equipment and improving cross-platform system function.

##### (v) Strengthen risk control and guarantee sound development of business

In 2013, the Board of Directors performed ultimate duties of risk management positively, paid close attention to market changes, analyzed all risks in management and development carefully, continuously improve risk management system and guarantee stable operation of the Company. Firstly, the sensitivity of management of credit risks was improved. Initiative risk management was conducted centering upon “customer, product, industry and region” in accordance with supervision requirements to enhance function of orientation and control of policy and credit management method comply with requirements of the Company was formulated and management of credit risks was standardized and strengthened to effectively promote sound development of credit business of the Company on the basis of risk identification and management method established according to operational characteristics and risk features of small and medium-sized enterprises. The Company established “consultation mechanism for credit cooperation of stock customers” on the basis of adequate investigation along with Laiwu Branch of the Agricultural Bank of China and Rural Credit Cooperatives of the city and made in-depth cooperation in fields of credit customer maintenance, credit risk early warning and linkage disposal to further improve cooperation level between banks and play a positive role in jointly withstanding credit risks and prompting the development of

real economy in Laiwu. Secondly, the management of related transactions was strengthened. The Board of Directors further strengthened management of related transaction, positively improved management mechanism of related transaction, optimized approval process of related transaction and conducted comprehensive Credit Extension for Laiwu Steel Group Co., Ltd., Xinwen Mining Group Co., Ltd., Shandong Taishan Steel Group Co., Ltd., Taifeng Textile Group Co., Ltd. and its affiliated enterprises. The management reported conditions of related transactions to the Board of Directors quarterly and adequately discussed and reviewed the fairness and risks of related transactions to strengthen management and control of risks in related transactions of the Company. Thirdly, the control level of information technology risks was improved. In 2013, the Information Technology Risk Committee of the Board of Directors required the management to continue to improve the control of information technology risks, to employ Ernst & Young Consulting Company for cooperation in the establishment of information technology risk management system of Laishang Bank, and to improve over 10 systems including Administrative Measures for Business Continuity of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. At the same time, the management of business continuity was strengthened and the coordinated management system responding to emergency and disaster was established, with business continuity as the objective. In addition, overall business continuity code and in-depth mechanism construction were further enhanced to effectively respond to accident and disaster and boost continuous and sound development of the Company. Fourthly, the management was guided to effectively resolve and write off non-performing loans. In 2013, in face of severe and complex economic situation and descending pressure, and ownership survived from wrote off account to effectively prevent "two height" of non-performing and provision. Other non-performing loans were provided with strict confirmation of responsibility and subdivision to truly reflect loan form and positively resolve and recover.

## (vi) Perform social responsibility positively and manifest social value of the enterprise

In 2013, the Board of Directors continued to perform social responsibility positively, enhance corporate culture construction, boost the Company to establish long-term mechanism of social responsibility, and show good social image of the Company. Firstly, benefits of stakeholders were maintained with all strength. All shareholders were adequately respected and treated fairly to create good return on investment for shareholders. Compliance and honesty management was insisted to continuously improve customer service experience and guarantee lawful rights and interests of depositors. Secondly, enterprises were guided to firm and develop confidence and bank and enterprises joined hands to pass the hardship. With supporting idea “financing and talent”, the 6th meeting of development forum of small and medium-sized enterprises themed as “existence, law, mastery” was held and governments, banks and enterprises jointly discussed development strategy of seeking opportunity in crisis. Main principals of over 600 small and medium-sized enterprises in four provinces and eleven cities were invited to attend and experts of the Ministry of Industry and Information Technology were specially employed to give lectures and answer questions raised by enterprises on the site, evoking strong reaction in entrepreneurs in all places. In addition, the Company holding the idea “help instead of neglect” positively helped enterprises trapped in management plight temporarily and implemented preferential interest rate to reduce financial costs of enterprises, helping a lot of enterprises to pass the difficulty. Thirdly, corporate culture construction was advanced stably. Enterprise Culture Department was added at the time of institutional reform.

and specialized company was employed to sort out culture system of the enterprise. In addition, sorting out of core idea of enterprise culture including core value, mission and vision was completed preliminarily and new corporate image promotion video was produced. Visual identification system elements such as trade badge, trade name and outdoor advertising were redesigned. Fourthly, public welfare establishments were highlighted as usual. In 2013, the Company continued to participate in one day donation of benevolence, Spring Buds Project, voluntary blood donation, Shandong women's volleyball of title sponsorship and volunteer activity of city construction in the whole city, donated RMB 2 million to set up "mother fund" and donated RMB 1 million to Lushan earthquake-stricken area. Social popularity and credibility of the Company were improved effectively and the Company was awarded with honorary title "Shandong demonstration enterprise performing social responsibility".
